We can implement a suite:
1. Initial constructions:
   - Normalize parent if present.
   - Power law q_j = binomial-like or (1+x+j0)^-0.5 with parameters shift, exponent 0.3-0.6, maybe with tail adjustment.

LP update algorithm details:
```
def lp_update(p, m, n, matrix_data):
   # solve max q sum s.t. A q <= m
   # to avoid unbounded if p has zeros, ensure p>=eps
   # use linprog with c=-ones
   res=linprog(c, A, b_ub=np.full(2n-1,m*1.000001), method='highs')
   if res.success:
      q=res.x
      q=np.clip(q,0,None)
      s=q.sum()
      if s>0: return q/s
```

A more powerful local method: **coordinatewise exact minimization**. For each i, if we change p_i and keep others fixed, the objective as a function of x is max over k of (c_k^0 + 2x * s_{k,i} + x^2 * δ_{k=2i?}) where s_{k,i}=∑? Actually c_k = ∑_j p_j p_{k-j}. If only p_i varies, contribution = p_i^2 if k=2i + 2 p_i ∑_{j≠i?} ... We can compute exact quadratic for each k and find minimum of max of quadratics on [0, sum others]. Could solve via ternary/binary. This is expensive for all i (n*2n). But can sample indices. It ensures coordinate descent. For a high-dimensional nonnegative simple convex? Function max of quadratics not convex (or maybe convex in x? Each quadratic coefficient for k=2i is 1 positive; others linear; max of convex? A max of linear and one convex quadratic is convex? Linear functions are both convex/concave; max of convex functions is convex. Each c_k as function of x (others fixed) is linear (if 2i≠k) or quadratic with positive coefficient if 2i=k. Linear is convex. Max of convex is convex! Indeed c_k(x)=2 x s_k + (x^2 if k=2i else linear constant). So max over k is convex in scalar x. We can minimize exactly on simplex with coordinate. Nice!    if other <=0: return x
   # define eval(z)

Coordinate minimization algorithm robust:
```
def _eval_z(delta, x, S, out_max, inside_base, p, c2):
    denom = S + delta
    if denom <= 1e-300: return inf
    val_inside = np.max(inside_base + 2.0*delta*p) if n>0 else 0
    val = max(val_inside, out_max)
    if c2 is not None:
       special = c2 + 2.0*delta*x + delta*delta
       if special > val: val = special
    return 2*n*val/(denom*denom)
```

```
def coord_eval(base, p, out_base, i, r, x, n):
    if x >= 1.0 - 1e-15:
        # only i nonzero; after update, vector proportional only i if others zero. score 2n (or inf if sum0)
        return 2.0*n if r>0 else np.inf
    lam = (1.0 - r) / (1.0 - x)
    lam2 = lam*lam
    cross = 2.0*lam*r
    vals = lam2 * base + cross * p
    if i < n:
        vals[i] = lam2 * base[i] + r*r
    # outside
    out_val = lam2 * out_base
    m = vals.max()
    if out_val > m: m = out_val
    return 2.0*n*m
```

Coordinate functions:
```
def make_coord_grid(size=40):
   # unique values in [0,1]
   lin = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, size)
   log = 10.0 ** np.linspace(-8, 0, size//2)
   log = np.clip(log, 0, 1)
   return np.unique(np.concatenate([lin, log, [0,1]]))
```
But global grid with 60? For vectorized memory. Use `GRID = np.unique(...)` at import? Top-level okay. But no nested. Let's set `COORD_GRID = make_coord_grid()`? If we call function at top-level after definition. But to avoid issues, in propose_candidate create grid and pass to functions.

`coord_eval_grid(base, p, out_base, i, rs, x, n)`:
Return scores array. Need p old a. If x >= 1-1e-12, maybe handle.
```
def coord_eval_grid(base, p, out_base, i, rs, x, n):
    if x >= 1.0 - 1e-12:
        return np.full(len(rs), 2.0*n) # not useful
    lam = (1.0 - rs) / (1.0 - x)
    lam2 = lam*lam
    cross = 2.0*lam*rs
    vals = lam2[:,None] * base[None,:] + cross[:,None] * p[None,:]
    if i < n:
        vals[:,i] = lam2 * base[i] + rs*rs
    m = vals.max(axis=1)
    outv = lam2*out_base
    np.maximum(m, outv, out=m)
    m *= 2.0*n
    return m
```
Potential memory: For each coordinate, `vals` G x n. G maybe 60; n=1024 ->61k floats 0.5MB; per coordinate okay but 1024 coords ->500MB allocated sequential, okay but overhead. Could reduce G=32. Let's select grid ~32. Use `COORD_GRID_SIZE=32`.

`coord_scalar_eval(base,p,out_base,i,r,x,n)`:
```
lam=(1-r)/(1-x); lam2=lam*lam; cross=2*lam*r
vals=lam2*base + cross*p
if i<n: vals[i]=lam2*base[i]+r*r
m=float(np.max(vals)); ov=lam2*out_base; if ov>m: m=ov
return 2*n*m
```

Coordinate sweep function:
```
def coordinate_polish(a, budget_s, rng, max_sweeps=None, reorder='random'):
    a=normalize(a); n=len(a)
    if n is None: return None, inf
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    current=2*n*np.max(c)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=current
    t0=time.monotonic()
    sweep=0
    grid=make_coord_grid()
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < budget_s:
       sweep+=1
       improved_this_sweep=0
       total_improve=0
       # order: random if rng else arange; could randomize per sweep
       idx=np.arange(n)
       if rng is not None: idx=rng.permutation(n)
       for i in idx:
          if time.monotonic()-t0 >= budget_s: break
          a,c,improved,new_score = try_coordinate(a,c,i,grid,n)
          if improved:
             current=new_score
             total_improve += (current - new_score)? Actually improvement previous-current
             if current < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score=current; best_a=a.copy()
             improved_this_sweep+=1
       # periodic recompute
       if sweep % 10 == 0:
          s=a.sum()
          if s>0:
             f=1/s; a*=f; c*=f*f
          c=np.convolve(a,a) # direct
          current=2*n*np.max(c)
          if current < best_score: update best
       if improved_this_sweep == 0:
          # maybe try a few random coordinates with wider search? coordinate already full. break if early? 
          if sweep > 3: break
       if sweep % 50 == 0: print progress
    return best_a, best_score
```

LP implementation in detail:
```
def lp_direction(a, budget? maybe one iteration):
   n=len(a); S=a.sum(); p=a/S
   c=np.convolve(p,p); m=max(c)  # normalized m (max c)
   # ensure p positive small
   eps=1e-12
   p_lp = np.maximum(p, eps)
   p_lp = p_lp / p_lp.sum()
   m = max(np.convolve(p_lp,p_lp))  # recompute to feasible
   # Build A dense n? Constraints: A[k,i] = p_lp[k-i] valid. A shape (2n-1,n)
   b_ub = np.full(2*n-1, m*(1+1e-9)+1e-15)
   c_obj = -np.ones(n)
   res = linprog(c_obj, A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0,None), method='highs')
   q = res.x
   q = np.clip(q,0,None)
   s=q.sum()
   if s<=1e-12: return None
   q_norm=q/s
   return q_norm
```
But if p has zeros clipped eps, m slight. Use direct conv for p_lp.

A=np.empty((2*n-1,n))
for k in range(2*n-1):
   s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n-1,k)
   if s<=e:
       A[k,s:e+1]=p_lp[k-e:k-s+1]
```

Let's implement exact score for candidates:
```
def exact_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,None); S=a.sum()
   if S<1e-10: return np.inf
   conv=np.convolve(a,a)
   return 2*len(a)*float(np.max(conv))/(S*S)
```

def normalize_array(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<=1e-12: return None

def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
       hs = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
       if hs is not None:
          hs=np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64)
          hs=np.clip(hs[~np.isnan(hs) & ~np.isinf(hs)],0,1000)
          if len(hs)>0: return hs
    except: pass
    return None
```

Signature:
```
def try_coordinate(a,c,i,grid,n):
    x = float(a[i])
    one_minus_x = 1.0 - x
    if one_minus_x <= 1e-12: return np.inf, False
    c_len = 2*n -1
    base = c[i:i+n] - 2.0*x*a
    if i < n:
        base[i] = base[i] + x*x
    # out_base
    left = 0.0
    if i > 0: left = float(np.max(c[:i]))
    right = 0.0
    if i+n < c_len: right = float(np.max(c[i+n:]))
    out_base = max(left,right)
    current_score = 2.0*n*float(np.max(c))  # could be passed to avoid recompute? pass current_score.
```
Pass current_score to avoid np.max(c) per coordinate. But after no update, current_score unchanged. We can pass `current_score`. However, if c has changed from previous update, current_score is known. Use parameter.
`try_coordinate(a,c,i,grid,n,current_score)`.

Grid evaluation:
```
rs = grid
# If x not near extremes, vectorized
lam = (1.0 - rs) / one_minus_x
lam2 = lam*lam
cross = 2.0*lam*rs
vals = lam2[:,None]*base[None,:] + cross[:,None]*a[None,:]
# diag
vals[:,i] = lam2*base[i] + rs*rs
m = vals.max(axis=1)
ov = lam2*out_base
np.maximum(m, ov, out=m)
m *= 2.0*n
# combine with current best
if current_score < float(m.min()): best_r = float(x)? We need include x. current_r=x, current_score.
best_score = current_score; best_r = float(x)
better = m < best_score
if any: best_score=float(m.min()); best_r=float(rs[argmin])
```

Local golden interval:

Build dense A:
```
def make_conv_matrix(p,n):
   K=2*n-1
   A=np.empty((K,n), dtype=np.float64)
   for k in range(K):
      s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n-1,k)
      if s<=e:
         A[k,s:e+1]=p[k-e:k-s+1]
   return A
```
This is top-level. For LP, p positive `eps=1e-9` and renormalize. Constraints: A q ≤ m, q≥0. `b_ub` = m*(1+1e-8)+1e-12.

`central_binom_coeffs(n)`:

`make_power(n, alpha, side='left', rng=None, shift=1.0)`:
```
t=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
if side=='left': a=(t+shift)**(-alpha)
elif side=='right': a=(n-1-t+shift)**(-alpha)
elif side=='both': a=((t+shift)**(-alpha)+ (n-1-t+shift)**(-alpha))**0.5
return normalize
```
For side='left', q-like but less structured. Include alpha 0.4,0.5,0.6.

```

S=a.sum(); if S<1e-12: return None
return a/S
```

def make_coord_grid(size=24):

def coordinate_polish(a_init, budget_s, rng=None, grid=None, golden_iters=20, max_n=4096, verbose=False, recompute_every=8):
```
If len(a)>max_n, return after score? For safety.
At start, if `a_init` is list, normalize. If None return.
`c = np.convolve(a,a) if n<=2500 else fft_convolve`.
`current = 2*n*np.max(c)`; best_a=a.copy(); best_score=current.
If current is inf/nan, maybe return.
While time < t_end:
  sweep++
  idx = np.arange(n); if rng: rng.shuffle(idx) else maybe random? Use `idx = rng.permutation(n) if rng is not None else np.arange(n)`.
  sweep_improvements=0
  for i in idx:
     if time > t_end: break
     new_score, improved = try_coordinate(a,c,i,grid,n,current, golden_iters)
     if improved:
       current=new_score; sweep_improvements +=1
       if current < best_score: best_score=current; best_a=a.copy()
  if sweep % recompute_every ==0:
     s=sum(a); if s>0 factor=1/s; a*=factor; c*=factor*factor
     c=np.convolve(a,a) if n<=2500 else fft_conv
     # due exact recompute, update current and best
     current=2*n*np.max(c)
     if current < best_score: best_score=current; best_a=a.copy()
  if verbose and sweep%20==0: print
  if sweep_improvements ==0:
     # Try maybe 3 random coordinate with wider? Or break.
     if sweep > 5: break
  # If best_score very close? no.
return best_a,best_score
```

def lp_search(p_init, budget_s, rng=None):

Detailed `try_coordinate` code:
```
def try_coordinate(a, c, i, grid, n, current_score, golden_iters=20):
    x = float(a[i])
    one_minus_x = 1.0 - x
    if x < 0.0:
        x = 0.0; a[i]=0.0
    if x > 1.0:
        # normalize? return
        return current_score, False
    if one_minus_x <= 1e-10:
        return current_score, False
    c_len = 2*n - 1
    base = c[i:i+n] - 2.0*x*a
    if i < n:
        base[i] = base[i] + x*x
    if i > 0:
        left = float(np.max(c[:i]))
    else:
        left = 0.0
    start_out = i + n
    if start_out < c_len:
        right = float(np.max(c[start_out:]))
    else:
        right = 0.0
    out_base = left if left > right else right
    rs = grid
    # If grid size zero, skip
    if rs.size == 0:
        return current_score, False
    lam = (1.0 - rs) / one_minus_x
    lam2 = lam * lam
    cross = 2.0 * lam * rs
    # Use multiplication. If n==0 no.
    vals = lam2[:, None] * base[None, :] + cross[:, None] * a[None, :]
    vals[:, i] = lam2 * base[i] + rs * rs
    m = vals.max(axis=1)
    outv = lam2 * out_base
    np.maximum(m, outv, out=m)
    m *= 2.0 * n
    best_r = float(x)
    best_sc = float(current_score)
    # Also check if any NaN? If m contains nan, ignore.
    finite = np.isfinite(m)
    if np.any(finite):
        idx = int(np.argmin(np.where(finite, m, np.inf)))
        sc = float(m[idx])
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc; best_r = float(rs[idx])
    # Golden refinement around best_r
    if golden_iters > 0:
        if best_r <= 1e-8:
            lo = 0.0
            hi = min(1.0, max(1e-6, best_r * 10.0 + 0.05))
        elif best_r >= 1.0 - 1e-8:
            lo = max(0.0, 1.0 - 0.05)
            hi = 1.0
        else:
            lo = max(0.0, best_r * 0.6)
            hi = min(1.0, best_r * 1.4)
            if hi - lo < 1e-4:
                lo = max(0.0, best_r - 0.025)
                hi = min(1.0, best_r + 0.025)
        if hi > lo:
            # ensure current x? if x outside and better? not needed
            r_star, sc_star = golden_coord(base, a, out_base, i, lo, hi, x, n, golden_iters)
            if sc_star < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_star; best_r = r_star
    if best_sc + 1e-12 >= current_score:
        return current_score, False
    # If best_r far? clamp
    if best_r < 0.0: best_r=0.0
    if best_r > 1.0: best_r=1.0
    if abs(best_r - x) < 1e-14:
        return current_score, False
    one_minus_x = 1.0 - x
    if one_minus_x <= 1e-10: return current_score, False
    lam = (1.0 - best_r) / one_minus_x
    lam2 = lam * lam
    cross = 2.0 * lam * best_r
    # Update c
    old_a = a.copy()
    old_c = c.copy()
    new_seg = lam2 * base + cross * old_a
    new_seg[i] = lam2 * base[i] + best_r * best_r
    c[i:i+n] = new_seg
    if i > 0:
        c[:i] *= lam2
    if i + n < c_len:
        c[i+n:] *= lam2
    old_a[:i] *= lam
    old_a[i] = best_r
    old_a[i+1:] *= lam
    a[:] = old_a
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s > 1e-300:
        f = 1.0 / s
        a *= f
        c *= f * f
    # Clip tiny negatives
    if np.any(a < 0.0):
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
        ss = float(np.sum(a))
        if ss > 0:
            a /= ss; c /= ss*ss
        else:
            a[:] = old_a; c[:] = old_c; return current_score, False
    new_score = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(new_score) or new_score + 1e-9 >= current_score:
        a[:] = old_a; c[:] = old_c
        return current_score, False
    return new_score, True
```
Note: We copied `old_a` before updating c, but we assign `a[:] = old_a` after modifying `old_a`. `old_c` copy. Good. But we used `old_a` in new_seg before modifying; okay. We saved old_a before c update? Yes `old_a=a.copy()`; then c update uses old_a. Good. If update fails, revert a,c to old copies. But `a[:] = old_a` after old_a modified? We need original old_a for c evaluation. If update fails, we need original a, not updated. We lost original because we overwrote old_a with updated values. Save separate `orig_a = a.copy(); orig_c = c.copy();` and use `a_work = a`? Simpler:
```

# use a (unchanged) for new_seg

# update a,c
...
if fail: a[:] = orig_a; c[:] = orig_c
```

`golden_coord` top-level:
```
def golden_coord(base, a, out_base, i, lo, hi, x, n, iters):
    invphi = 0.6180339887498949
    c1 = hi - invphi*(hi-lo)
    c2 = hi - (1.0-invphi)*(hi-lo)
    f1 = coord_scalar_eval(base,a,out_base,i,c1,x,n)
    f2 = coord_scalar_eval(... c2)
    for _ in range(iters):
       if f1 < f2:
          hi=c2; c2=c1; f2=f1
          c1=hi-invphi*(hi-lo)
          f1=coord_scalar_eval(...)
       else:
          lo=c1; c1=c2; f1=f2
          c2=hi-(1-invphi)*(hi-lo)
          f2=coord_scalar_eval(...)
    return 0.5*(lo+hi), min(f1,f2)
```

`coord_scalar_eval`:
```
def coord_scalar_eval(base, a, out_base, i, r, x, n):
    x = float(x); one=1.0-x
    if one <= 1e-10: return np.inf
    lam=(1.0-r)/one
    lam2=lam*lam
    cross=2.0*lam*r
    vals=lam2*base + cross*a
    if i<n:
        vals[i]=lam2*base[i]+r*r
    m=float(np.max(vals))
    ov=lam2*out_base
    if ov>m: m=ov
    return 2.0*n*m
```
Could use `np.max(vals, initial=-inf)`? For n>0.

def make_coord_grid(size=17):
    lin = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,int(size))
    log = 10.0 ** np.linspace(-6.0,0.0,int(size*0.5)+1)
    vals=np.concatenate([lin,log,[0.0,1.0]])
    vals=np.clip(vals,0.0,1.0)
    return np.unique(vals)
```

def exact_score(a, conv=None):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); S=a.sum()
   if S<1e-12: return inf
   n=len(a)
   if conv is None:
      if n<=3000: conv=np.convolve(a,a)
      else: conv=fft_convolve(a,a)
   return float(2*n*np.max(conv)/(S*S))
```

def select_top_candidates(candidates, k, unique_length=False):
    selected=[]
    used_lengths=set()

Let's write `fft_convolve`:
```
def fft_convolve(a,b):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); b=np.asarray(b,float)
   n=a.size; m=b.size
   size=1
   while size < n+m-1: size <<=1
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,size)
   return np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,size)[:n+m-1]
```
For `a` same as b, okay.

`central_binom(n)`: if n<=0 None. q sum maybe. Normalize inside or return normalized. We can return normalized.

```
def central_binom(n):
   q=np.empty(n); q[0]=1.0; for i in range(1,n): q[i]=q[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
   s=q.sum(); if s>0 q/=s
   return q
```

`make_factor_q(n,m,alpha)`:
```
q=central_binom(n)
a=q.copy()
if m>0 and m<n:
   a[m:] += alpha*q[:n-m]
return normalize_array(a)
```
If alpha=0. Use central_binom returns normalized, but adding alpha and renormalize okay.

t=np.arange(n,float)
if side=='left': a=(t+1.0)**(-alpha)
elif side=='right': a=(n-1.0-t+1.0)**(-alpha)
else: a=np.sqrt((t+1.0)**(-alpha)+(n-1.0-t+1.0)**(-alpha))
return normalize
```

`solve_lp_once`: 
```
def solve_lp_once(p,n):
   if linprog is None: return None
   p=np.asarray(p,float); p=np.clip(p,1e-9,None); s=p.sum(); p/=s
   conv=np.convolve(p,p) if n<=3000 else fft_conv
   m=float(np.max(conv))
   if m<=0 or not finite: return None
   A=build_lp_matrix(p,n)
   b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, m*(1+1e-8)+1e-12)
   c_obj=-np.ones(n)
   try:
      res=linprog(c_obj,A_ub=A,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=(0,None),method='highs',options={'presolve':True})
   except TypeError:
      res=linprog(...)
   except Exception:
      return None
   if res is None: return None
   if not getattr(res,'success',False):
       # If optimal with status 0? linprog success true.
       pass
   if not getattr(res,'success',False): return None
   q=np.asarray(res.x,float)
   if q is None or q.size!=n: return None
   q=np.clip(q,0,None)
   s=q.sum()
   if s<1e-10: return None
   q/=s
   return q
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The main optimizer is a strong scale-invariant coordinate descent:
in one move, coordinate i is changed to a new value r in [0,1] while
all other coordinates are scaled proportionally so that the total sum
remains 1. For a fixed i, the resulting objective is a 1-D function
of r; it is minimized with a coarse grid plus golden-section refinement.

The program also uses analytic initializations (quadratic/binomial-like,
power-law, factor perturbations), random restarts, and optionally an
LP-inspired high-dimensional update if SciPy/HiGHS is available.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def fft_convolve(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = b.size
    size = 1
    while size < n + m - 1:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, size)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, size)[:n + m - 1]


def exact_score(a, conv=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    n = a.size
    if conv is None:
        if n <= 3000:
            conv = np.convolve(a, a)
        else:
            conv = fft_convolve(a, a)
    mx = float(np.max(conv)) if conv.size else 0.0
    if mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def normalize_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 1e-12:
        return None
    return a / s


def central_binom(n):
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    q = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    q[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        q[i] = q[i - 1] * (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
    s = float(np.sum(q))
    if s > 0.0:
        q /= s
    return q


def make_factor_q(n, m, alpha):
    q = central_binom(n)
    if q is None:
        return None
    a = q.copy()
    if 0 < m < n:
        a[m:] += alpha * q[:n - m]
    return normalize_array(a)


def make_power(n, alpha, side):
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if side == "left":
        a = (t + 1.0) ** (-float(alpha))
    elif side == "right":
        a = (n - 1.0 - t + 1.0) ** (-float(alpha))
    else:
        a = np.sqrt((t + 1.0) ** (-float(alpha)) + (n - 1.0 - t + 1.0) ** (-float(alpha)))
    return normalize_array(a)


def resample_seq(seq, n):
    seq = normalize_array(seq)
    if seq is None:
        return None
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if seq.size == n:
        return seq
    if seq.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    xd = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, seq.size)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(xn, xd, seq)
    return normalize_array(out)


def random_perturb(base, sigma, rng):
    base = normalize_array(base)
    if base is None:
        return None
    n = base.size
    noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), size=n))
    a = base * noise
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-12, None)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 1e-12:
        return None
    return a / s


def make_coord_grid(size=17):
    lin = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, max(5, int(size)))
    logn = max(3, int(size * 0.6))
    logv = 10.0 ** np.linspace(-8.0, 0.0, logn)
    vals = np.concatenate([lin, logv, np.array([0.0, 1.0])])
    vals = np.clip(vals, 0.0, 1.0)
    vals = np.unique(vals)
    return vals


def coord_scalar_eval(base, a, out_base, i, r, x, n):
    one = 1.0 - float(x)
    if one <= 1e-10:
        return np.inf
    lam = (1.0 - float(r)) / one
    lam2 = lam * lam
    crs = 2.0 * lam * float(r)
    vals = lam2 * base + crs * a
    if 0 <= i < n:
        vals[i] = lam2 * base[i] + float(r) * float(r)
    mx = float(np.max(vals)) if vals.size else 0.0
    ov = lam2 * float(out_base)
    if ov > mx:
        mx = ov
    if mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mx)


def golden_coord(base, a, out_base, i, lo, hi, x, n, iters):
    if hi <= lo:
        return 0.5 * (lo + hi), coord_scalar_eval(base, a, out_base, i, 0.5 * (lo + hi), x, n)
    lo = float(lo)
    hi = float(hi)
    phi = 0.6180339887498949
    c1 = hi - phi * (hi - lo)
    c2 = hi - (1.0 - phi) * (hi - lo)
    f1 = coord_scalar_eval(base, a, out_base, i, c1, x, n)
    f2 = coord_scalar_eval(base, a, out_base, i, c2, x, n)
    for _ in range(max(0, int(iters))):
        if f1 < f2:
            hi = c2
            c2 = c1
            f2 = f1
            c1 = hi - phi * (hi - lo)
            f1 = coord_scalar_eval(base, a, out_base, i, c1, x, n)
        else:
            lo = c1
            c1 = c2
            f1 = f2
            c2 = hi - (1.0 - phi) * (hi - lo)
            f2 = coord_scalar_eval(base, a, out_base, i, c2, x, n)
    if f1 <= f2:
        return c1, f1
    return c2, f2


def try_coordinate(a, c, i, grid, n, current_score, golden_iters=20):
    """
    Try to improve coordinate i while all other coordinates are scaled
    proportionally. Modifies a and c in place only on a successful update.
    Returns (updated_score, updated_flag).
    """
    x = float(a[i])
    if x < 0.0:
        x = 0.0
    if x > 1.0:
        return current_score, False
    one_minus_x = 1.0 - x
    if one_minus_x <= 1e-10:
        return current_score, False

    c_len = 2 * n - 1

    # base = convolution coefficients with the contribution of coordinate i removed
    base = c[i:i + n] - 2.0 * x * a
    if 0 <= i < n:
        base[i] = base[i] + x * x

    if i > 0:
        left = float(np.max(c[:i])) if c[:i].size else 0.0
    else:
        left = 0.0
    start_out = i + n
    if start_out < c_len:
        right = float(np.max(c[start_out:])) if c[start_out:].size else 0.0
    else:
        right = 0.0
    left = max(0.0, left)
    right = max(0.0, right)
    out_base = left if left > right else right

    rs = grid
    if rs.size == 0:
        return current_score, False

    lam = (1.0 - rs) / one_minus_x
    lam2 = lam * lam
    crs = 2.0 * lam * rs

    # All candidate r-values simultaneously.
    vals = lam2[:, None] * base[None, :] + crs[:, None] * a[None, :]
    if 0 <= i < n:
        vals[:, i] = lam2 * base[i] + rs * rs
    mx = vals.max(axis=1)
    ov = lam2 * out_base
    np.maximum(mx, ov, out=mx)
    np.maximum(mx, 0.0, out=mx)
    mx *= float(2.0 * n)

    best_r = float(x)
    best_sc = float(current_score)

    finite = np.isfinite(mx)
    if np.any(finite):
        idx = int(np.argmin(np.where(finite, mx, np.inf)))
        sc = float(mx[idx])
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_r = float(rs[idx])

    if golden_iters > 0:
        if best_r <= 1e-8:
            lo = 0.0
            hi = min(1.0, max(1e-6, best_r * 10.0 + 0.05))
        elif best_r >= 1.0 - 1e-8:
            lo = max(0.0, 1.0 - 0.05)
            hi = 1.0
        else:
            lo = max(0.0, best_r * 0.6)
            hi = min(1.0, best_r * 1.4)
            if hi - lo < 1e-4:
                lo = max(0.0, best_r - 0.025)
                hi = min(1.0, best_r + 0.025)
        if hi > lo:
            r_star, sc_star = golden_coord(base, a, out_base, i, lo, hi, x, n, golden_iters)
            if np.isfinite(sc_star) and sc_star < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_star
                best_r = float(r_star)

    if best_sc + 1e-12 >= current_score:
        return current_score, False

    if best_r < 0.0:
        best_r = 0.0
    if best_r > 1.0:
        best_r = 1.0
    if abs(best_r - x) < 1e-14:
        return current_score, False

    one_minus_x = 1.0 - x
    if one_minus_x <= 1e-10:
        return current_score, False

    lam = (1.0 - best_r) / one_minus_x
    lam2 = lam * lam
    crs = 2.0 * lam * best_r

    orig_a = a.copy()
    orig_c = c.copy()

    new_seg = lam2 * base + crs * a
    if 0 <= i < n:
        new_seg[i] = lam2 * base[i] + best_r * best_r
    c[i:i + n] = new_seg

    if i > 0:
        c[:i] *= lam2
    if i + n < c_len:
        c[i + n:] *= lam2

    if i > 0:
        a[:i] *= lam
    a[i] = best_r
    if i + 1 < n:
        a[i + 1:] *= lam

    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s > 1e-300:
        f = 1.0 / s
        a *= f
        c *= f * f

    if np.any(a < 0.0):
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
        ss = float(np.sum(a))
        if ss > 1e-300:
            a /= ss
            c /= (ss * ss)
        else:
            a[:] = orig_a
            c[:] = orig_c
            return current_score, False

    new_score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
    if new_score < 0.0:
        new_score = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(new_score) or new_score + 1e-9 >= current_score:
        a[:] = orig_a
        c[:] = orig_c
        return current_score, False

    return new_score, True


def coordinate_polish(a_init, budget_s, rng=None, grid=None, golden_iters=20, max_n=4096):
    a = normalize_array(a_init)
    if a is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return None, np.inf
    if n > max_n:
        return None, np.inf

    if grid is None:
        grid = make_coord_grid(16)

    if n <= 3000:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        c = fft_convolve(a, a)

    cur = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cur):
        return a, np.inf
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = cur

    if budget_s <= 1e-6:
        return best, best_score

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    tend = t0 + float(budget_s)
    sweep = 0
    recompute_every = 8

    while time.monotonic() < tend:
        sweep += 1
        idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        if rng is not None:
            idx = rng.permutation(n)

        improved_in_sweep = 0
        g_iter = golden_iters
        if n <= 768:
            g_iter = max(g_iter, 20)
        elif n <= 1536:
            g_iter = max(12, golden_iters - 6)
        else:
            g_iter = max(8, golden_iters - 12)

        for ii in idx:
            if time.monotonic() >= tend:
                break
            new_score, improved = try_coordinate(a, c, int(ii), grid, n, cur, g_iter)
            if improved:
                cur = new_score
                improved_in_sweep += 1
                if cur < best_score:
                    best_score = cur
                    best = a.copy()

        if sweep % recompute_every == 0:
            s = float(np.sum(a))
            if s > 1e-300:
                f = 1.0 / s
                a *= f
                c *= f * f
            if n <= 3000:
                c = np.convolve(a, a)
            else:
                c = fft_convolve(a, a)
            cur = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
            if cur < 0.0:
                cur = 0.0
            if np.isfinite(cur) and cur < best_score:
                best_score = cur
                best = a.copy()
            if not np.isfinite(cur):
                return best, best_score

        if improved_in_sweep == 0 and sweep >= 6:
            break

        if sweep % 25 == 0:
            print(f"      coord_polish n={n} sweep={sweep} score={best_score:.7f} "
                  f"t={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s", flush=True)

    return best, best_score


def build_lp_matrix(p, n):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    K = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.empty((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(K):
        s = max(0, k - n + 1)
        e = min(n - 1, k)
        if s <= e:
            A[k, s:e + 1] = p[k - e:k - s + 1]
    return A


def solve_lp_once(p, n):
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    p = normalize_array(p)
    if p is None:
        return None
    if n > 1024:
        return None

    p_safe = np.clip(p, 1e-9, None)
    ss = float(np.sum(p_safe))
    if ss < 1e-12:
        return None
    p_safe /= ss

    if n <= 3000:
        conv = np.convolve(p_safe, p_safe)
    else:
        conv = fft_convolve(p_safe, p_safe)
    m = float(np.max(conv))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return None

    A = build_lp_matrix(p_safe, n)
    b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, m * (1.0 + 1e-8) + 1e-12, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    res = None
    try:
        res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0, None), method="highs",
                      options={"presolve": True})
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0, None))
        except Exception:
            res = None

    if res is None:
        return None
    success = bool(getattr(res, "success", False))
    if not success:
        st = getattr(res, "status", None)
        if st == 0:
            success = True
    if not success:
        return None

    q = np.asarray(getattr(res, "x", None), dtype=np.float64) if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None else None
    if q is None or q.size != n:
        return None
    q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(q))
    if s < 1e-10:
        return None
    return q / s


def lp_search(p_init, budget_s, rng=None):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    tend = t0 + max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    p = normalize_array(p_init)
    if p is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = p.size
    if n > 1024 or linprog is None:
        return p, exact_score(p)

    if n <= 3000:
        conv = np.convolve(p, p)
    else:
        conv = fft_convolve(p, p)
    cur = float(2.0 * n * np.max(conv))
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = cur
    iters = 0

    while time.monotonic() < tend - 20.0:
        q = solve_lp_once(p, n)
        if q is None:
            break

        mix_best = None
        mix_score = cur
        fractions = [1.0, 0.85, 0.7, 0.55, 0.45, 0.35, 0.25, 0.15, 0.08]
        for frac in fractions:
            cand = (1.0 - frac) * p + frac * q
            sc = exact_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < mix_score:
                mix_score = sc
                mix_best = cand

        if mix_best is not None and mix_score < cur - 1e-12:
            p = mix_best
            cur = mix_score
            iters += 1
            if cur < best_score:
                best_score = cur
                best = p.copy()
            print(f"      lp_update iter={iters} score={cur:.7f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s", flush=True)
        else:
            break

        if iters >= 50:
            break

    return best, best_score


def get_parent_sequence():
    obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if obj is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    return arr


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    t_global = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s) - 75.0)
    deadline = t_global + budget
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    grid = make_coord_grid(16)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf

    def note(seq_obj):  # local convenience; not nested for helper reuse? It is nested, so avoid by inlining below.
        pass

    # The prompt forbids nested closures/lambdas. Do not use the local function above.
    # We only defined it for drafting, but Python would create a closure. Instead we
    # implement explicit inline updates below. Delete local function usage.
    # (The empty local function is harmless but avoid closures entirely.)

    parent_raw = get_parent_sequence()
    parent = None
    if parent_raw is not None:
        if parent_raw.size <= 2048:
            parent = normalize_array(parent_raw)
        else:
            parent = resample_seq(parent_raw, 1024)
        if parent is not None:
            sc = exact_score(parent)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = parent.copy()
            print(f"parent sequence n={parent.size} score={sc:.7f}", flush=True)

    # Build candidate pool.
    candidates = []

    lengths = []
    if parent is not None:
        lengths.append(parent.size)
    for nn in [256, 512, 768, 1024, 1536]:
        lengths.append(nn)

    seen = set()
    for nn in lengths:
        n = int(nn)
        if n < 2 or n > 2048 or n in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(n)

        bases = []
        q = central_binom(n)
        if q is not None:
            bases.append(q)

        f1 = make_factor_q(n, max(1, n // 3), 0.12)
        if f1 is not None:
            bases.append(f1)

        f2 = make_factor_q(n, max(1, n // 2), 0.06)
        if f2 is not None:
            bases.append(f2)

        p0 = make_power(n, 0.5, "left")
        if p0 is not None:
            bases.append(p0)

        if n <= 768:
            p1 = make_power(n, 0.35, "left")
            if p1 is not None:
                bases.append(p1)
            noise = random_perturb(central_binom(n), 0.07, rng)
            if noise is not None:
                bases.append(noise)

        if parent is not None:
            rs = resample_seq(parent, n)
            if rs is not None:
                bases.append(rs)

        for b in bases:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 180.0:
                break
            a = normalize_array(b)
            if a is None:
                continue
            sc = exact_score(a)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                continue
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = a.copy()
                print(f"initial n={a.size} score={sc:.7f} (new best)", flush=True)
            else:
                print(f"initial n={a.size} score={sc:.7f}", flush=True)
            candidates.append((sc, a.size, a))

    # Quick coordinate polish of best initial candidates.
    quick_total = min(600.0, max(60.0, budget * 0.22))
    if candidates and time.monotonic() < deadline - 240.0:
        # Select top candidates manually (no lambda).
        rem = list(range(len(candidates)))
        num_quick = min(8, len(rem))
        quick_each = min(70.0, max(8.0, quick_total / max(1, num_quick)))
        for _ in range(num_quick):
            if not rem:
                break
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 150.0:
                break
            bi = rem[0]
            for idx in rem:
                if candidates[idx][0] < candidates[bi][0]:
                    bi = idx
            seq = candidates[bi][2]
            rem.remove(bi)

            a, sc = coordinate_polish(seq, quick_each, rng, grid, golden_iters=12)
            if a is None:
                continue
            sc2 = exact_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc2):
                if sc2 < best_score:
                    best_score = sc2
                    best_seq = a.copy()
                    print(f"quick polish n={a.size} score={sc2:.7f} (new best)", flush=True)
                else:
                    print(f"quick polish n={a.size} score={sc2:.7f}", flush=True)
                candidates.append((sc2, a.size, a))

    # Long coordinate polish of the current best.
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0:
        long_budget = min(1400.0, max(250.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.52))
        a, sc = coordinate_polish(best_seq, long_budget, rng, grid, golden_iters=20)
        if a is not None:
            sc2 = exact_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                best_score = sc2
                best_seq = a.copy()
                print(f"long coord polish n={a.size} score={sc2:.7f} (new best)", flush=True)
            else:
                print(f"long coord polish n={a.size} score={sc2:.7f}", flush=True)

    # Optional LP-inspired phase for moderate lengths.
    if (linprog is not None and best_seq is not None and len(best_seq) <= 1024 and
            time.monotonic() < deadline - 400.0):
        lp_budget = min(450.0, max(80.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.25))
        a, sc = lp_search(best_seq, lp_budget, rng)
        if a is not None:
            sc2 = exact_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                best_score = sc2
                best_seq = a.copy()
                print(f"LP phase n={a.size} score={sc2:.7f} (new best)", flush=True)
            else:
                print(f"LP phase n={a.size} score={sc2:.7f}", flush=True)

            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0:
                a2, sc3 = coordinate_polish(a, min(120.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 70.0),
                                            rng, grid, golden_iters=20)
                if a2 is not None:
                    sc4 = exact_score(a2)
                    if np.isfinite(sc4) and sc4 < best_score:
                        best_score = sc4
                        best_seq = a2.copy()
                        print(f"post-LP coord n={a2.size} score={sc4:.7f} (new best)", flush=True)

    # Random restarts / perturbations.
    restart_sigmas = [0.03, 0.07, 0.12, 0.22, 0.38]
    restart_count = 0
    while best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 130.0 and restart_count < 10:
        sigma = restart_sigmas[restart_count % len(restart_sigmas)]
        a = random_perturb(best_seq, sigma, rng)

        if a is not None and restart_count % 4 == 3:
            trial_n = [512, 768, 1024][restart_count % 3]
            b = resample_seq(best_seq, trial_n)
            if b is not None:
                a = b
                a = random_perturb(a, sigma * 0.6, rng)

        if a is None:
            restart_count += 1
            continue

        alloc = min(220.0, max(45.0, (deadline - time.monotonic() - 80.0) / 3.0))
        a, sc = coordinate_polish(a, alloc, rng, grid, golden_iters=16)
        if a is not None:
            sc2 = exact_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                best_score = sc2
                best_seq = a.copy()
                print(f"restart {restart_count} n={a.size} score={sc2:.7f} (new best)", flush=True)
            else:
                print(f"restart {restart_count} n={a.size} score={sc2:.7f}", flush=True)
        restart_count += 1

    # Final small polish if time remains.
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        final_budget = min(120.0, max(5.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 18.0))
        a, sc = coordinate_polish(best_seq, final_budget, rng, grid, golden_iters=20)
        if a is not None:
            sc2 = exact_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                best_score = sc2
                best_seq = a.copy()

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)

    # Scale to a safe nontrivial sum.
    a = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(np.sum(a))
    a = a * (10.0 / s)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    final_score = exact_score(a)
    print(f"FINAL score={final_score:.7f} n={a.size}", flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in a]
```
